([Rameezuddinmohammed/counterX](https://github.com/Rameezuddinmohammed/counterX/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/counterx-reviewer.md))
- **counterx-security**: Audits and investigates CounterX's security and trust boundaries — authentication, tenant/scope isolation, secrets and signing-key handling, CTP envelope integrity, row-level security, kill switches, and the invariants listed in CLAUDE.md.
